# Script Results Index

Maps every script in `04-computation/` to its stored output.
Results are stored in `05-knowledge/results/` with matching filenames (`.out` extension).

**Convention:** When you run a script, save its output:
```bash
python3 04-computation/SCRIPT.py 2>&1 | tee 05-knowledge/results/SCRIPT.out
```

---

| `lrc_goddyn_wong_sporadic_tilers_codex_s106.py` | [STORED] | HYP-2893 Goddyn-Wong sporadic-tiler audit.  Encodes the acceleration criterion: replacing `v` in `{1,...,n}` by `2v` remains tight when every integer in `[n-v+1,2n-2v+1]` has nontrivial gcd with `v`.  Exact envelope checks verify selected rows `n=7,13,19,32,73` have `M=1/(n+1)`; exact safe measure is `0` for `n<=32`.  The LRC14 row is `n=13,v=12`, window `[2,3]`; the infinite one-speed subfamily is `n≡1 mod 6`, and the `n=73` multi-acceleration tail `...,69,71,73,140,144` is exact-verified. | `lrc_goddyn_wong_sporadic_tilers_codex_s106.out` |
| `lrc14_unital_pair_slot_realizability_codex_s107.py` | [STORED] | HYP-2894 guardrail for the q=3 unital pair-slot lead.  Enumerates the 11 `S8` orbit types of four-edge subsets of `K8` and proves no `S8`-invariant union realizes a `2-(28,4,1)` design on the `28=C(8,2)` edge slots: zero unions have 63 blocks with lambda 1 on both adjacent and disjoint edge-pairs.  AP8 sum/difference packet classes are nonuniform, so the unital is a weighted frame after a category-1 labelling, not a canonical AP pair-slot design.  The carrier tournament ranks AP/Goddyn-Wong exact-tiling and AP sum/difference packets above the unital frame, and places the refuted `S8`-invariant identification last. | `lrc14_unital_pair_slot_realizability_codex_s107.out` |
| `lrc14_clebsch_bruhat_unital_carriers_codex_s105.py` | [STORED] | HYP-2891 finite carrier audit from the unital/Clebsch/truncated-octahedral prompts.  Verifies Clebsch as folded 5-cube `(16,5,0,2)` and its closed-neighborhood `2-(16,6,2)` pair design; the tangent-sector quotient maps 64 LRC residual masks to 16 Clebsch classes, four masks each, but every class mixes missed depth, so it is a signed covariance carrier rather than a scalar `q_t` quotient.  Verifies the truncated-octahedral/Bruhat `S4` graph has 24 vertices, 36 edges, 6 commutation squares, 8 braid hexagons, automorphism group size 48, and two edge orbits (outer swaps vs middle swaps).  Emits the proof target: decompose HYP-2890 residual leak over tangent Clebsch classes and Bruhat square/hexagon faces. | `lrc14_clebsch_bruhat_unital_carriers_codex_s105.out` |
| `lrc14_design_hodge_carriers_codex_s105.py` | [STORED] | HYP-2892 design/Hodge refinement for the LRC14 residual leak, subordinate to HYP-2891's labelled Clebsch/Bruhat quotient.  Constructs the `q=3` Hermitian unital over `GF(9)` (`28` points, `63` four-point secant blocks, point replication `9`, pair replication `1`, incidence frame `N^T N=8I+J`), matching the `C(8,2)=28` k=8 AP pair slots.  Verifies the Clebsch folded-cube SRG `(16,5,0,2)` and its closed-neighborhood `2-(16,6,2)` frame `N^T N=4I+2J`.  Constructs the Bruhat/truncated-octahedral `S4` graph (`24` vertices, `36` adjacent-transposition edges, `6` square commutation faces, `8` hex braid faces, cycle rank `13`) and the HYP-2887 octahedral `L(K4)` support-six carrier.  Guardrail from KPS S31m/S31n: this is not a sparse-design exact-tiler proof; it is a pair-frame/curl carrier after the HYP-2890 same-frequency packet is removed, while AP/dilates and Goddyn-Wong remain category-1 tight-locus objects. | `lrc14_design_hodge_carriers_codex_s105.out` |

| `lrc14_gamma_frequency_tail_codex_s104.py` | [STORED] | HYP-2890 same-frequency additive-energy tail and residual-leak target.  Extends KPS S31k from `m=1` to all same-frequency additive quadruple modes, with `Gamma_k(m)=C_{k,r}/m^4` on residues `r mod 7`; all constants are positive for k=8..13 and `H=12` leaves absolute tail `<=1.084e-6` at k=8/9.  The full packet overpredicts AP, so the live inequality is the residual leak `R_sf(E)-R_sf(AP)<=Gamma_sf(A*(AP)-A*(E))`; exact anchored scans have 0 violations for k=8 (`3432` rows, worst ratio `0.469`) and k=9 (`3003` rows, worst ratio `0.933`, row `(0,2,4,6,7,8,10,12,14)`). | `lrc14_gamma_frequency_tail_codex_s104.out` |

### How to bulk-capture results

Run this to capture results for any script:
```bash
for f in 04-computation/*.py; do
    base=$(basename "$f" .py)
    if [ ! -f "05-knowledge/results/${base}.out" ]; then
        echo "Running $f..."
        timeout 300 python3 "$f" > "05-knowledge/results/${base}.out" 2>&1
    fi
done
```

Note: Some scripts take >5 minutes. Use `timeout` appropriately.

## Convention for new scripts

1. Save the script in `04-computation/`
2. Run it and pipe output to `05-knowledge/results/SCRIPT.out`
3. Add a row to this INDEX
4. If the result confirms/refutes a hypothesis, update `05-knowledge/hypotheses/INDEX.md`
